# Tasks: Assistant data scope

**Spec:** [spec.md](spec.md) (v1.7) · **Plan:** [plan.md](plan.md) (revision 10) · **Contract:** [contracts/chat-api.md](contracts/chat-api.md)

Format: `- [ ] T### [P] [US#] Description (path/to/file)`. `[P]` = parallel with other `[P]` tasks in the same batch (different files, no shared dependency). IDs T066–T068 were added in plan revision 2; T069–T070 in revision 3; T071 in revision 4 (removed in revision 6, ID kept); T072 in revision 5; T073–T077 in revision 6 (unit-first cadence, spec v1.6); T078–T079 in revision 8 (spec v1.7; T025 dropped, ID kept); T080 in revision 9 (analyze round 1 of spec v1.7, split out of T079). They sit where they run.

**Batches:** each `Batch` is one implement/review cycle ending in a **Review checkpoint**. Sizes include tests. Batch table and worktree map: [plan.md Batches](plan.md#batches).

**Gate per batch (F1b to 6c):** `npx tsc --noEmit` and `npm run test:unit`, nothing else. The reviewer re-runs only these two. No e2e run before Batch E, with one exception: the targeted 3a2 merge run (Review checkpoint 3a2).

**Global rules:**
- **Unit first.** Every logic check (no browser, DB or server needed) is a unit test in `tests/unit/*.test.ts` (flat directory, plan D10), shown red before its code; that red unit run is the fail-first proof. Unit tests import only the pure modules listed in plan D10 and build data with `memDb()`.
- **e2e still written, not run.** Each task writes its e2e tests (UI, HTTP contract, a11y, focus, typing, navigation, chat-vs-dashboard parity, and one or two representative rows per logic REQ through `ask()`). They must type-check per the tsc rule below. They run first in Batch E (single exception: `npx playwright test assistant-parity.spec.ts -g "dashboard"` once at the 3a2 merge, user decision 2026-10-29; see Review checkpoint 3a2); UI-only and HTTP-only behavior gets its fail-first proof there (T077).
- **While iterating, run only what you touch:** `npx tsx --test tests/unit/<file>.test.ts` for the unit files of the code being changed. The full `npm run test:unit` runs once per batch, at the checkpoint.
- **Batch size ≤ ~450 changed lines including tests** (≈ the old ~300 src-only target; in 1a2 tests were ~64% of the lines: +330/-26 tests vs +119/-82 src, 557 total vs 360 planned). Estimates in the batch headers and plan Batches count tests (revision 7: previous src-only estimates × ~1.6). Done over the old target and accepted by the user 2026-10-29: 1a1 (~436), 1a2 (~557). Splits proposed in revision 7 are conservative (plan Batches).
- **Added vs deleted lines (after T013: +187/-989 vs ~360 estimated, 454 of them one whole-file deletion):** estimates and the ~450 cap count ADDED lines; deleted lines are tracked in a separate column. A whole-file deletion or rewrite task is its own batch.
- **Parallel rule (user, 2026-10-29):** when a reviewer reviews code the next code-writer phase will not modify or use, that phase starts in parallel with the review (in a worktree if the reviewer's tsc/test runs could be disturbed; on the same branch if the file sets are disjoint). If the review finds a problem in files the parallel phase touched, the orchestrator merges the fixes afterwards. Planner/spec edits (tasks.md, plan.md) always run in parallel with code work; the orchestrator commits docs path-limited.
- **Running estimate:** when a batch's running count of added lines (tests included) passes its estimate by more than ~50 lines, the implementer stops at the next task boundary, commits, and reports; the rest becomes a follow-on batch.
- **Split test tasks:** where a batch is split between two code tasks that one test task covers, the test task is split by the code task its rows cover ("part 1", "part 2"); each part is committed just before its code, so every batch tip is tsc- and unit-green.
- **Worktrees only where the plan marks batches independent:** F1b beside 1a1 (from the U0 tip), and 3a1–3a2 beside 1c1–3b2 (from the 1b2 tip, merged before 3c). Worktrees are created from the current feature-branch tip, only write code and run tsc and unit tests (never e2e), commit path-limited per task, need `node_modules` (a symlink is fine), and are merged back by the orchestrator by cherry-pick or `git merge --no-ff` (no fast-forward once the main line advanced), which reruns tsc and `npm run test:unit` on the merged tip. If one side must edit a file the other owns, both run sequentially instead. Everything else is sequential: chat.ts, chat-intent.ts, chat-meals.ts and chat-metrics.ts are shared.
- **e2e runs** (Batch E and Final only) are sequential and in the foreground (one cloned DB, port 3200); never two at once, never in the background, nothing else running.
- **tsc rule:** `npx tsc --noEmit` must pass at each task's final commit and at every batch checkpoint. A red tests-only commit is exempt but is never left as the batch tip or a worktree's merge tip (plan Testing strategy). **Red window (decided after 1a2):** red tests are committed per code task, immediately before that task's code, so the tsc-red window is one commit; a batch does not commit all its red tests up front (1a2 did: db42b49 left tsc red until 1b3be66).
- **Grep gates over old specs:** gates that look for removed names search `src` plus the old specs only: tests/e2e/assistant-{api,answers,next-step,a11y,composer,look}.spec.ts, tests/e2e/assistant-scope.spec.ts (until T013 deletes it), tests/e2e/theme-palette.spec.ts and tests/e2e/assistant-helpers.ts. The new negative-assertion files tests/e2e/assistant-noscope.spec.ts and tests/unit/chat-answer.test.ts are excluded explicitly; negative assertions on removed names live only there.
- Commits are path-limited and per task, tests first then code, the test commit separate from and just before its code commit (red window rule above); messages cite US, T and REQ IDs. Commit a task before the next task touches the same file; never `git add -A`. No `specs/` in commits unless the user asks.
- Never run `next build` in place, never add packages (spec v1.7: none; T025 dropped), no migrations.
- Copy is verbatim from spec Copy (es-MX).
- `resolved` (test-only, plan D2) may be asserted while a builder is pending, but no REQ is covered by it alone; each REQ's text assertion is listed in the Coverage table.
- **Known failures on main** (not this feature): `dashboard.spec.ts:91`, `dashboard.spec.ts:213` (data-dependent), and the `a-theme-visual` cart snapshot. Listed in the Batch E and Final notes. (Batch E: neither dashboard row failed in runs 1 or 2; the cart snapshot was refreshed in T057 with the user's yes and stays fragile, see Batch note E.)
- **Snapshots:** one refresh only, T057 at the end of Batch E, after the user's explicit yes. Confinement to the assistant panel/FAB or the dashboard search bar (REQ-4) is judged by the reviewer from the diff image, per test name.
- **prompt-kit attribution** (copied or adapted files): `// Adapted from prompt-kit (https://github.com/ibelick/prompt-kit), MIT License` plus the verbatim copyright line.

**Batch note F1b (done; T005–T007, commits bd537de, 10f9b28, 9536424 in worktree branch worktree-agent-a961a3030b18fbcc7):** `testHooks(headers, env)` is active only when `E2E_HOOKS === "1"` and `NODE_ENV !== "production"`; it parses `x-e2e-today` (strict YYYY-MM-DD, a real calendar day), `x-e2e-fail: chat` and `x-e2e-trace: 1`. Route: a JSON parse or zod failure → `badRequest` (no log); everything after → 500 `{ error: "Internal error" }` with `console.error("[api/rag/chat]", { userId, messageLength }, error)`. Client: `ApiError(message, status)`; the error turn records `server: status === 500`. Unproven until Batch E: assistant-errors.spec.ts (500 status and body, the log format `messageLength:\s*N`, the 2 s fixed waits for "no log entry", the `page.route` injections). Any e2e run before Batch 1b2 (e.g. the 3a2 targeted run if it touches these files) shows the old-copy failures listed in T013 (500 mocks in assistant-next-step, -a11y, -answers, -look).

Plurals live in the matcher (entry, +s, +es, z→ces; allowance computed on the singular length); `*` stems exact prefix only, never fuzzy; never-fuzzy words exact-only as input and as entries; first letter must match; OSA distance (swap = 1 edit); 22 table rows run. Accepted: `ñ` compares as `n` (spec Terms), so año/ano and caña/cana collide. Decimals split under `normalizeText` ("1,5 L" → "1 5 l"): number and date parsing must not rely on `words` for decimals (noted in T039, T040, T043, T045). **Open questions 1c2 (for the user; the current behavior stays until answered):**
1. "receta del pollo" / "ingredientes del pollo": the spec lists only "de" as a dish cue, so "del" is not one. Add "del" (and "de la")?
2. "nombre del plan" / "¿Cómo se llama el plan?" resolve to Plan, not Identity (REQ-31 plan name). Should they be Identity?
3. "¿A qué hora ceno?" shows all meal times because "ceno" (4 letters) is exact-only and is not a listed slot word. Add verb forms (ceno, desayuno, como) as slot words?
4. "recetas de pollo" becomes a dish cue, so after T079 it is a not-found/did-you-mean on "pollo" rather than a recipe-list filter. Is that the intended reading? Note (analyze round 1, L11): spec v1.7 says "Open questions: None", so this item is not a spec gap on record yet; T026 part 2 pins the current reading as CURRENT behavior pending the user's answer, and the answer is recorded in spec.md at the next spec-change (with the 1d1/1d2 lists).
5. The `compr*` stem makes "comprendo" a cart question. Narrow the stem (e.g. compra*, compro, comprar)?
6. Never-fuzzy words are exact-only only in their listed form: "objetos" (plural of never-fuzzy "objeto") is not on the list, so it fuzzy-matches "objetivo" and returns Goals. Should the never-fuzzy list also cover plurals (objetos, pasos, peros)?

The resolver reads an ordinal word before or after a slot word, or a number right after one ("colación 2", "segunda colacion", "colación segunda"). `chooseSlot` takes an optional ordinal: on a duplicated slot it picks that slot; out of range it re-asks ("Tienes 2 colaciones. ¿Cuál?"); on a single slot it is ignored. The 2a1 dead end (M3, duplicate-slot buttons looping on the clarify) is fixed; options-version buttons still reach the D3 placeholder until T032. Plural "snacks" maps like "snack". **Pre-note 3b2 (open user ruling, carry-forward (b) of Batch note 3b1; needed before T067's rows are written):** the parser ignores trailing tokens after a recognized phrase, so these give silent wrong answers: "el lunes pasado" → the latest Monday; "el miércoles pasado" on a Wednesday → today; "desde el lunes" → that Monday only; "desde ayer" → yesterday only; "esta semana pasada" → this week; "en el mes de enero" → this month; "hoy y ayer" → today only; "desde el 5 de noviembre de 2024" ignores the year. Singular "últimos 1 día" is unrecognized while N ≤ 1 maps to today. The spec's REQ-14 table is silent on all of these. Reviewer's recommendation: return `"unrecognized"` (so the fallback line shows) for weekday + "pasado"/"pasada", "desde" + a non-date, and an explicit year after "desde el D de <mes>". Needs a spec-change or a one-line user ruling; T067 then adds a row per ruled case.

**Batch note 3b2 (done; T067, T068; commits on top of 4ce6b7b: e88b489 T067 red (file-level red: the new exports are missing; tsc-exempt), da80a5e T068 code; tip da80a5e; tsc clean, unit 642/642; no dependency change; only src/lib/domain/chat-dates.ts and tests/unit/chat-dates.test.ts changed; +151/-10 (src +67/-8, tests +84/-2) vs ~350 estimated; reviewer PASS, no HIGH, no fix list):**

- What landed (chat-dates.ts): `periodLabel(period, today)`; `clampPeriod(period, today)` → `{ period, clamped }`; `CLAMP_LINE` and `notUnderstoodLine(periodo)` with the spec Copy verbatim. Label rules per the REQ-14 table: single-day kinds `wd d mon`; ranges of 7 days or less `wd d`; longer ranges `d mon`; the end reads "hoy" when it is today; the year is added for a date outside today's year. Clamp: a span above 366 days becomes `lastDays` for the last 366 with `clamped` true (400 clamps from its raw bounds; N above the 3660 cap clamps the same; exactly 366 is not clamped); a future `to` is cut at today without `clamped`. `phraseAt` can return `"unrecognized"`.
- USER RULING 2026-11-12 (given in this session when asked; resolves Pre-note 3b2 and 3b1 carry-forward (b)): period phrases with extra date-like words return `"unrecognized"` instead of silently answering another period. Implemented for: weekday + pasado/pasada ("el lunes pasado", "el miércoles pasado", "el jueves pasada"); "desde" + a non-date ("desde ayer", "desde el lunes"); an explicit 4-digit year right after the month in "desde el D de <mes>"; "esta semana pasada"; "en el mes de <mes>"; exact "hoy y ayer". Plain forms still work. **This ruling is a session decision, NOT yet written into spec.md v1.7** (spec is approved): add it to REQ-14 at the next spec-change, together with the other pending items (HANDOFF section 6), incl. the clamp wording "any N above 366 clamps" (3b1 (a)) and LOW-1 below.
- Wiring: `periodLabel`/`clampPeriod`/`CLAMP_LINE`/`notUnderstoodLine` are wired into answers by T042/T043 (water, exercise) and T044/T045 (weight, meals); not a dead-export issue.
- Proof: 25 mutations run against the full unit suite, all killed except one equivalent no-op (`dayNumber + 0`). The red was file-level only (missing exports), so the mutations stand in for per-row red proof.
- Carry-forwards from the review:
  - **MEDIUM-1 (DONE in 3b3, d20363c; user answer 2026-11-12, see Batch note 3b3):** the broadened "desde" rule (chat-dates.ts:73-79) swallows a later valid phrase: "desde luego, ¿cuánta agua tomé hoy?" and "desde mi casa, ¿cuánta agua tomé ayer?" went from correct (today/yesterday) to `"unrecognized"`; order-dependent ("ayer, desde mi casa" still gives yesterday). Reviewer's narrower fix: `return undefined` when the token after "desde" is not date-shaped (hoy, ayer, antier, a weekday, optional "el" plus a number or weekday), so the final cue loop still yields `"unrecognized"` when nothing else matches.
  - **MEDIUM-2 (DONE in 3b3, b7ebcb7; user answer 2026-11-12, see Batch note 3b3):** "desde el 5 de noviembre del 2024" and "…del año pasado" still silently give `since 2025-11-05`: the filter drops only "el" and "de", so "del" bypasses the year guard (colloquial wording of the ruled year case).
  - LOW-1 (spec gap, record in REQ-14 at the next spec-change): a short range crossing a year boundary names the month on both ends and adds the year to the foreign-year date ("La semana pasada (lun 29 dic 2025 – dom 4 ene)" on 2026-01-07): the code-writer's choice, not in the spec. A short range crossing a month boundary inside the year stays ambiguous under the literal rule ("La semana pasada (lun 26 – dom 1)" on 2026-11-04; "Este mes (dom 1 – hoy)" on 2026-03-01).
  - LOW-2 (leftover silent shapes, not ruled; list for the spec-change): "ayer y hoy", "ayer y antier", "hoy y mañana", "hoy y el lunes", "el lunes y martes", "la semana pasada y esta", "últimos 7 días y ayer", "este mes pasado", "este mes de enero", "el pasado lunes" (on a Monday gives today rather than 7 days back), "desde el 5 de noviembre hasta el 10 de noviembre" (gives since), "comida pasada del lunes", "hoy pasado", "ayer o el lunes", "la semana pasada el lunes", "desde el 5 de noviembre hasta hoy"; singular "últimos 1 día" is unrecognized.
  - LOW-3 (OPEN, left as is in 3b3; false positives of the new guards): "el lunes, pasada la comida" is unrecognized (the comma is dropped in tokenization); "desde el 5 de noviembre, 1500 kcal" is unrecognized (any 4-digit token after the month trips the year guard). Fix together with MEDIUM-1/MEDIUM-2.
  - LOW-4 (DONE in 3b3, b7ebcb7): `MONTH_NAMES` (chat-dates.ts:113) duplicates `MONTHS` (line 12); reuse `MONTHS` (`WEEKDAY_NAMES` is legitimately separate for accents). Fold into the MEDIUM-1/2 fix or T043.
  - LOW-5 (DONE in 3b3, 41b47d3): no row pins the "desde" fallback with a later valid phrase, "del 2024", or the negative guard cases ("pasado mañana" and "el año pasado" stay `"unrecognized"`); add rows (tests/unit/chat-dates.test.ts) when MEDIUM-1/2 are decided, red first.

  - **DOM contract (from 6a1 review MEDIUM-3; the T055 tests assert exactly this, do not deviate):**
    - Every `.assistant-turn` (user and assistant) contains an `.assistant-bubble`; on assistant turns the avatar sits beside the bubble. The turn is the full-width row; the bubble carries the look.
    - Move the border and fill rules from `.assistant-turn` to `.assistant-bubble`: the fills in assistant-chat.css (~82-83) and the forced-colors rule `.assistant-turn { border: 1px solid CanvasText }` (~126).

      - `button[data-variant="option"]`: block buttons with layout `"option"`; stretch to the bubble's inner width (clientWidth minus padding); ≥44 px tall; a long label wraps and the button grows; no ellipsis, no clipping.
      - `data-variant="chip"`: answer-level question buttons (e.g. "Mi cena de hoy").
      - `a[data-variant="link"]`: navigation links are real anchors (next/link), ≥44×44 px; the lucide `ArrowUpRight` (class `lucide-arrow-up-right`; installed lucide-react 0.468.0 emits `lucide-${kebab}`) is the LAST child, with `aria-hidden="true"`.
    - The accessible name of links and options equals their label.
    - Question buttons exist only on the latest answer. A nav link on an old answer still navigates, and the old answer stays.
    - While a request is pending, question buttons are `aria-disabled="true"` with NO native `disabled`, stay focusable, and the handler ignores activation (a forced click sends nothing).
    - Tab order is DOM order: options, then the link, then the chip. Bubbles have no tabindex.
    - Contrast: boundary = the better of fill vs an OPAQUE border against the log ≥3:1 (a translucent border does not count); bubble text ≥4.5:1; avatar icon and the arrow ≥3:1. Both themes.

- **6b1 fixes:** MEDIUM 1 `onComplete` fires once per text (a `completedIdentity` ref compared with `identity`, `NOT_YET` symbol; React 19 strict mode keeps refs across the simulated unmount/remount; an `instant` flip after completion does not re-fire; it never fires only if unmounted mid-typing, which is fine). MEDIUM 2 midpoint and negative-elapsed rows (break-and-restore killed round, ceil, sqrt, weakened clamp, missing clamp, `split("")`, UTF-16 slicing, wrong break condition, dropped highlights). LOW 3 `useState(() => skip ? length : 0)` with `skip = instant || useMediaQuery(reduced) || length === 0`: no frame at 0; no hydration risk (turns start `[]`, answers arrive after a fetch). LOW 4 `Intl.Segmenter` graphemes for length, duration and clipping (emoji/ZWJ kept whole; a cluster split across two spans is clipped between spans: documented, harmless).
- **T060, what landed:** (a) `Lead` renders the full lead with `<RichText>` plus an overlay `<RichText rich={clipRich(rich, typed)}>` (`<strong>` for highlights; not nested in a `<p>` block; extra `.assistant-lead-text` wrapper). (b) Typed-once flag: module-level `WeakSet<ChatAnswer>` (`typedAnswers`) keyed by the answer object (a retry's new answer still types); only `blocks[0].lead` types. (c) The real lead stays at full length with `opacity:0` under an `aria-hidden` overlay in the same grid cell (`.assistant-turn-lead[data-typing]`); sections, sources and later blocks keep their space at `opacity:0`; `.assistant-actions` and `.assistant-chips` are `visibility:hidden; opacity:0` while the article has `data-typing`, so layout and `scrollHeight` are final from frame 1; `transition` covers only opacity, so `visibility` flips at once and buttons are focusable immediately while the fade plays. (d) The live region holds the real full text (announced content unchanged while typing); the overlay is `aria-hidden`; per block a `<span className="sr-only">3 opciones: A; B; C</span>` ("1 opción" singular, labels joined with "; "), matching the spec's `("3 opciones: …")`; no double announcement; block buttons stay outside the region. (e) `onComplete` adds the answer to `typedAnswers`; `data-typing` derived from `typed < length`; fades `transition: opacity 160ms` on `.assistant-turn-later`, `.assistant-actions`, `.assistant-chips`; `transition: none` under reduced motion (same specificity (0,1,0), later in source order). (f) Tap to complete: `onClick` on `.assistant-bubble` sets `tapped`, so `instant` is true (acceptable on a non-interactive div: keyboard users have the live region and buttons are hidden while typing). (g) Duration `min(900 ms, graphemes × 18 ms)` (800 ms since 1c079f3, user decision 2026-11-13). AssistantTurn.tsx gained `"use client"` and is split into `AssistantTurn` (user branch) and `AssistantReply` (hooks). New CSS class `.assistant-turn-later`; new attribute `data-typing` on the article (the only DOM-contract addition).
- **Replay risk, reviewer-verified:** no replay on restore. The thread lives only in React `useState` (`useAssistantState` in src/components/app/assistant.tsx), with no sessionStorage/localStorage for it; AppShell in `(dashboard)/layout.tsx` persists across client navigations; `settle()` stores the API answer once and nothing clones it; a hard reload empties the thread. Residual harmless cases: Fast Refresh in dev resets the WeakSet; if thread persistence is ever added, restored answers would replay unless they are also marked typed; an answer that lands while the sheet is closed types unseen (panel `display:none`).
- **2-block grouping (6a2 MEDIUM): KEPT and ACCEPTED.** All block buttons stay after all block text in one `.assistant-actions`. Reviewer: accept (REQ-24/REQ-34 are silent on placement; one live region with buttons outside it is required by REQ-33 and chat-polish REQ-17, so interleaving would need one region per block; mild readability cost in 2-block answers). Accepted by default; the user may overrule and ask for per-block rows (HANDOFF section 6a).
- **T059, what landed (written, type-checked, NOT run):** assistant-look.spec.ts describe "REQ-33": a long lead is fully shown within 1 s (`end - arrival <= 1000`), typed count never goes down, `scrollHeight` constant in every frame, buttons hidden and sections at opacity 0 while typing then shown, one overlay in a 2-block answer, fades ≤200 ms (computed `transition-duration`), tap to complete <300 ms, reduced motion shows everything on the first frame with a 0 fade, no replay on close/reopen or navigation (a `MutationObserver` counting typing events over 1.2 s). assistant-a11y.spec.ts REQ-17: the announced text (aria-hidden excluded) changes once and contains the lead and "3 opciones: …" exactly once; no `a`/`button` in the region. Typing frames are sampled in-page with requestAnimationFrame (`traceTyping`, `waitForTyping`). The forced-colors "bubbles keep an edge" test also asserts an opaque border colour (6a2 LOW 4); the tops-in-ascending-order check uses log-relative offsets (a11y:~880 hardening); `ui.assistantTurns` now excludes `[data-typing]` (every count assertion waits out the typing; `ui.typing` added for the new tests); typo fixed in the 80% comment. Reviewer: no duplicate-text strict-mode risk (the overlay holds only a partial string and unmounts in the same render as the full text appears; the only lead-text locators are assistant-next-step.spec.ts 269, 305 and assistant-meals.spec.ts 113: fine), no hang risk (reduced-motion/instant never set `data-typing`; rAF under `clock.install()`; it would hang only if rAF were throttled, e.g. a hidden page), no vacuity risk.
